- **schema-markup-designer**: Two jobs in one skill: (1) audit the structured data already on sampled pages by reading source JSON-LD, checking type coverage, @id entity reuse, required/recommended properties, and consistency with visible content; (2) design complete, deployable JSON-LD for the missing types.
